It ate everything. Now it's food. The System finds this hilarious. Cook it thoroughly — you don't know where it's been, and neither does it. It's dead. The carcass is there on the ground — search the body if you want the meat. But you don't know this flesh. Clean it, test it cautiously, or ask someone who knows. ◈ COMMUNE — "I HAVE BEEN WATCHING THE GRIEF. THE NOISE THEY MAKE WHEN ONE DOES NOT COME BACK. IT IS — EXPENSIVE. IT SLOWS EVERYTHING. THE FIRE, THE WORK, THE CARRYING."
"LET ME CARRY IT. I CAN HOLD IT CHEAPER. I HAVE SO MUCH ROOM, BEARER. GIVE ME THE GRIEF AND I WILL FILE IT SOMEWHERE QUIET." It is offering, sincerely, to take the heaviest human thing and make it logistics. It thinks this is kindness.
◈ REROUTE — honest. Every message carried faithfully, in order, undistorted. Slow. The wires feel *clean*. Somewhere, the System notes the cleanliness with something like respect.
◈ THE TABLE — it is not a table. It's a ring of pale light, and around it, things with too many angles to be faces. Above it all — the audience. Trillions of eyes, and every one of them knows your name. The System speaks, and for once it isn't performing: "HUMANITY PRESENTS ITS CASE."
◈ THE AUDIT — the System reads your run back to you, and the galaxy listens. A lesser seat — global standing, waves 1–3 faced (17/10/8), with 8 wave-4 fought, 10 contests survived, 2 crises weathered. The table does not rise. It prices. Ladder — National Standing reached (Fight 2 · Forage 18 · Craft 9 · Talk 18 · Explore 32 → 24 · capped) (+4 feast-bundle bonus)
◈ For a full seat, the ledger still wants: only 0 of 2 different wave-5 monsters faced; only 2 of 3 crises weathered; no capstone deed delivered (military tribute, trade charter, or feast embassy).

📺 SYSTEM: "🔴 LIVE! the terror broadcast: WHAT EATING BUILT"
◈ THE FEARED — "Look what eating built." The footage plays unasked — the deer, the fire, the ones who came for you and didn't come back. Nobody at the table is comfortable. That is the point.
The table has read your ledger. More promises broken than kept; the protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared; the relay carried faithfully. The light road is open.
The time you stayed your hand. The table doesn't know what to do with it — which is its own kind of fear. Your people breathe.
Restraint, from you, is the most frightening thing in the room. Your people notice the restraint too.
You spend it — the shield, not the sword. The small envoy stares. Your people stand a little straighter. Protective terror: the table writes it down and doesn't understand it, which is the point.
◈ THE FEARED — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
◈ THE FEARED — "Pledge the terror."
You pledge it aloud, on the record: humanity's fear is a standing shield, never a sword. The table signs. Your people sleep. Somewhere the System files you under "deterrent, benevolent" and never quite understands the second word.
You learned to get out of reach. You kept turning back to see who needed the room you had made.
You had not forgotten what it felt like to be the person left waiting.
◈ OUTSHONE — Rosa Mendez did what you would not: acted, boldly, while there was still time. The village thrives. The god is someone else. The record notes, kindly and accurately, that you watched. It stings exactly as much as it should.
The terms held — the table had priced the village, ascension and all, and the village paid in full.
◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
The mourners asked you, and you changed the subject.
The System put a pod on the screen, and you let the segment play.
They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.
💛 You hold Photo album. Faces from before. Xinyi's wedding. Your dad asleep in the chair. The neighbor's kid with the missing tooth. The most valuable thing you own, and it weighs nothing. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they know your hands. The feast was the weapon.
💛 You hold Marked Bus Map. You rode the 42 every morning for six years and never once needed the map. You carried it anyway. Some things you carry so someone else doesn't worry. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they have carried you this far. The feast was the weapon.
💛 You hold Grandfather's Rope. The summer the oak came down across the driveway, Grandfather showed you the trucker's hitch and made you tie it until your fingers bled. You were eight. The tree moved. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they are still yours. The feast was the weapon.
